# @illus/core contracts v1.3 (approved by Shubham)

> Generated by `pnpm contracts` from `packages/illus-core`. Do not edit by hand.
> Deviations from and additions to spec §5 are explained in `docs/adr/0003-contract-decisions.md`; later amendments in the changelog below.
> Any change after the freeze needs an ADR and a version bump (spec §4).

1. [Data types](#1-data-types): every exported type, expanded (Zod schemas carry the same name as their type).
2. [Model output schemas](#2-model-output-schemas): JSON Schema of each `SchemaName`, with limits.
3. [API reference](#3-api-reference): interfaces, functions and constants (TypeDoc).

Test helpers (`@illus/core/testing`) are described in `packages/illus-core/README.md`.

## Changelog

### v1.3 (2026-10-09, [ADR 0030](adr/0030-w0-wave3-contracts.md))

- Optional DOM-free `TemplateSpec.targets(props): string[]` exposes valid §11 targets; every returned string must parse as `Target`. Optional `initialFrame(props)` returns pointers and containers using the corresponding `FrameState` field types. These hooks match W5's implementations and let W6 validate targets and seed frame 0 without importing React. Existing template implementations remain valid.
- `make.templateIR()` now uses array pointers `[{ name: 'majority', index: 1 }]`, with evidence paths `values` and `pointers.0`, matching W5's array props schema.
- Reader Chromium E2E runs in CI after the production build, in replay mode with throwaway test credentials. Renderer visual tests remain deferred until Linux baselines are generated and approved; darwin screenshots are not used as Linux baselines.

### v1.2 (2026-10-09, [ADR 0008](adr/0008-contracts-v1-1.md))

- `Block.text` of `kind: 'code'` blocks keeps line breaks and indentation (tabs included); only trailing whitespace per line and leading/trailing blank lines are trimmed. Every other kind still collapses whitespace (§6 step 9). Grounding stays whitespace-insensitive: quote and block are both collapsed before matching (§16), so a code quote matches with any spacing. W1 implements it in the indexer (wave 2). `docs/spec.md` is not edited.

### v1.1 (2026-10-09, [ADR 0008](adr/0008-contracts-v1-1.md))

Additive; every v1.0 producer and consumer stays valid.

- `TemplateSpec.dropElement?(props, path)`: optional. Returns props without the element at a `TemplateEvidence.path`, so `validate()` can drop an ungrounded template element (W5 implements, W10 calls).
- `VisualRecord.spec`: for IR kinds it holds `null` or the IR again. `@illus/render` compiles the validated IR to engine input itself (`toMermaid`, `toVegaLite`, `graphLayoutInput`, …); the graph layout is cached in `VisualRecord.layout`. Compilers stay in `@illus/render`.
- Spec §10 wording, as amended: **Mermaid ids are aliases** (`n0`, `n1`, … in IR order) that `@illus/render` maps back to IR ids; rendered elements carry `data-ir-id` and `data-target` with the IR id, never the alias. `docs/spec.md` is not edited; this entry and ADR 0008 are the record.
- `@illus/llm/server` is a public entry point (lint `ENTRY_POINTS`); no other deep import is allowed.
- `ROLES` is checked with an identity helper instead of `satisfies` (same type); `satisfies` is banned in `@illus` sources while Next 12's SWC compiles them ([ADR 0009](adr/0009-reader-build-on-typescript-5.md)).
- Deferred to the final gate: Linux visual baselines for `@illus/render` and Playwright/renderer e2e in CI. Until then `pnpm e2e` runs locally (darwin baselines) and CI runs lint, typecheck, unit tests and `next build`.

### v1.0 (2026-10-09, [ADR 0003](adr/0003-contract-decisions.md))

Initial freeze.

## 1. Data types

### `src/flags/index.ts`

```ts
type FlagName = "critic" | "templates" | "animations" | "tracer" | "conceptModels" | "htmlFallback"

type Flags = {
    readonly critic: boolean;
    readonly templates: boolean;
    readonly animations: boolean;
    readonly tracer: boolean;
    readonly conceptModels: boolean;
    readonly htmlFallback: boolean;
}

/** Partial overrides as stored in the `settings` store under key `flags`; unknown keys are ignored. */
type FlagOverrides = {
    critic?: boolean | undefined;
    templates?: boolean | undefined;
    animations?: boolean | undefined;
    tracer?: boolean | undefined;
    conceptModels?: boolean | undefined;
    htmlFallback?: boolean | undefined;
}
```


### `src/schemas/animation.ts`

```ts
/** An addressable element of a validated IR or template instance (§11). */
type Target = `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`

type HighlightTone = "success" | "focus" | "warning" | "muted"

/** One animation action (§11). Unknown targets or unsupported actions fail at frame-build time. */
type Action = {
    op: "highlight";
    target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
    tone: "success" | "focus" | "warning" | "muted";
} | {
    op: "unhighlight";
    target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | "all";
} | {
    op: "reveal";
    target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
} | {
    op: "hide";
    target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
} | {
    op: "set";
    target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
    value: string | number;
} | {
    op: "swap";
    a: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
    b: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
} | {
    op: "pointer";
    name: string;
    target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | null;
} | {
    op: "push" | "enqueue";
    container: string;
    id: string;
    value: string | number;
} | {
    op: "pop" | "dequeue";
    container: string;
} | {
    op: "add";
    element: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
    };
    parent?: string | undefined;
} | {
    op: "remove";
    target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
} | {
    op: "token";
    id: string;
    path: string[];
    label?: string | undefined;
} | {
    op: "annotate";
    target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
    text: string;
} | {
    op: "focus";
    target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | "all";
}

type ActionOp = "set" | "focus" | "highlight" | "unhighlight" | "reveal" | "hide" | "swap" | "pointer" | "push" | "enqueue" | "pop" | "dequeue" | "add" | "remove" | "token" | "annotate"

/** One step: narration ≤ 140 characters, grounded, ≤ 6 actions (§11). */
type Step = {
    id: string;
    narration: string;
    evidence: {
        blockId: string;
        quote: string;
        start?: number | undefined;
        end?: number | undefined;
        fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
    };
    actions: ({
        op: "highlight";
        target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
        tone: "success" | "focus" | "warning" | "muted";
    } | {
        op: "unhighlight";
        target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | "all";
    } | {
        op: "reveal";
        target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
    } | {
        op: "hide";
        target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
    } | {
        op: "set";
        target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
        value: string | number;
    } | {
        op: "swap";
        a: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
        b: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
    } | {
        op: "pointer";
        name: string;
        target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | null;
    } | {
        op: "push" | "enqueue";
        container: string;
        id: string;
        value: string | number;
    } | {
        op: "pop" | "dequeue";
        container: string;
    } | {
        op: "add";
        element: {
            id: string;
            label: string;
        };
        parent?: string | undefined;
    } | {
        op: "remove";
        target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
    } | {
        op: "token";
        id: string;
        path: string[];
        label?: string | undefined;
    } | {
        op: "annotate";
        target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
        text: string;
    } | {
        op: "focus";
        target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | "all";
    })[];
}

/** Output of the `animator` role (§11): 2–40 steps over an already-validated IR. */
type AnimationIR = {
    planItemId: string;
    initialHidden: (`node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`)[];
    steps: {
        id: string;
        narration: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
        actions: ({
            op: "highlight";
            target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
            tone: "success" | "focus" | "warning" | "muted";
        } | {
            op: "unhighlight";
            target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | "all";
        } | {
            op: "reveal";
            target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
        } | {
            op: "hide";
            target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
        } | {
            op: "set";
            target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
            value: string | number;
        } | {
            op: "swap";
            a: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
            b: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
        } | {
            op: "pointer";
            name: string;
            target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | null;
        } | {
            op: "push" | "enqueue";
            container: string;
            id: string;
            value: string | number;
        } | {
            op: "pop" | "dequeue";
            container: string;
        } | {
            op: "add";
            element: {
                id: string;
                label: string;
            };
            parent?: string | undefined;
        } | {
            op: "remove";
            target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
        } | {
            op: "token";
            id: string;
            path: string[];
            label?: string | undefined;
        } | {
            op: "annotate";
            target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
            text: string;
        } | {
            op: "focus";
            target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | "all";
        })[];
    }[];
}

/** Precomputed state of one animation frame (§11 reducer). `frames[0]` is the base spec with `initialHidden` applied (`step: 0`); `frames[i]` has `steps[0..i-1]` applied (`step: i`).  Persistence follows §11: highlights persist until `unhighlight`; pointers persist until moved or set to `null` (then the key is removed); `annotations` and `tokens` hold only the current step's. Records are keyed by `Target` string (or pointer name / container id) so frames stay JSON-serialisable. */
type FrameState = {
    step: number;
    hidden: (`node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`)[];
    removed: (`node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`)[];
    highlights: Record<string, "success" | "focus" | "warning" | "muted">;
    pointers: Record<string, `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`>;
    values: Record<string, string | number>;
    slots: Record<string, `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`>;
    containers: Record<string, {
        id: string;
        value: string | number;
    }[]>;
    added: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
        parent?: string | undefined;
    }[];
    annotations: Record<string, string>;
    tokens: {
        id: string;
        path: string[];
        label?: string | undefined;
    }[];
    focus: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | "all" | null;
    relayout: boolean;
    stepId?: string | undefined;
    narration?: string | undefined;
    evidence?: {
        blockId: string;
        quote: string;
        start?: number | undefined;
        end?: number | undefined;
        fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
    } | undefined;
}
```

### `src/schemas/book.ts`

```ts
/** Typed piece of a section (§5, classification rules in §6 step 6). */
type BlockKind = "quote" | "paragraph" | "code" | "figure" | "table" | "math" | "list" | "aside"

/** One block of book text. `text` is normalised plain text: NFC, soft hyphens removed, whitespace collapsed (§6 step 9). Exception since v1.2 (ADR 0008): for `kind: 'code'` it keeps line breaks and indentation (tabs included); only trailing whitespace on each line and blank lines at the start and end are trimmed. Grounding stays whitespace-insensitive: §16 collapses whitespace in quote and block before matching. */
type Block = {
    blockId: string;
    sectionId: string;
    kind: "quote" | "paragraph" | "code" | "figure" | "table" | "math" | "list" | "aside";
    text: string;
    cfiRange: string;
    label?: string | undefined;
    code?: {
        source: string;
        langHint?: string | undefined;
    } | undefined;
    figure?: {
        caption?: string | undefined;
        alt?: string | undefined;
        imgHref?: string | undefined;
    } | undefined;
    table?: {
        html: string;
        rows: string[][];
    } | undefined;
    math?: {
        mathml?: string | undefined;
        tex?: string | undefined;
    } | undefined;
}

/** Heading level that starts a section (h1–h4, §6 step 4). */
type SectionLevel = 1 | 3 | 4 | 2

/** A heading-delimited unit inside a spine item; the unit of generation (§5). */
type SectionNode = {
    sectionId: string;
    bookId: string;
    spineIndex: number;
    spineHref: string;
    ordinal: number;
    level: 1 | 3 | 4 | 2;
    title: string;
    headingPath: string[];
    childIds: string[];
    blockIds: string[];
    cfiStart: string;
    cfiEnd: string;
    contentHash: string;
    tokenEstimate: number;
    number?: string | undefined;
    parentId?: string | undefined;
    mergedInto?: string | undefined;
}

/** A link or textual reference from a block to a section or block (§5, §6 step 13). */
type CrossRef = {
    fromBlockId: string;
    kind: "link" | "textual";
    resolved: boolean;
    id?: number | undefined;
    label?: string | undefined;
    targetSectionId?: string | undefined;
    targetBlockId?: string | undefined;
}

/** Indexing progress of a book (§6): `structural` after phase A, `complete` after phase B. */
type IndexStatus = "none" | "structural" | "complete"

/** 150-word book summary plus naming conventions, the cached prompt prefix (§6 phase B step 6). */
type BookSummary = {
    summary: string;
    conventions: string[];
}

/** Record of the `books` store (§5). `bookId` = first 16 hex of SHA-256 of the EPUB bytes. */
type BookMeta = {
    bookId: string;
    title: string;
    indexStatus: "none" | "structural" | "complete";
    indexVersion: number;
    createdAt: number;
    updatedAt: number;
    sectionCount?: number | undefined;
    summary?: {
        summary: string;
        conventions: string[];
    } | undefined;
}
```

### `src/schemas/common.ts`

```ts
/** Id of an element inside one IR (node, edge, message, state, class…). Model-generated, so it is constrained to a short slug that is safe as a Mermaid id and as a `data-ir-id` value (§10, §13). */
type IrId = string

/** Display label: at most 40 characters, using the book's own terms (§8). */
type Label = string

/** SHA-256 as 64 lowercase hex characters. */
type Sha256Hex = string

/** Milliseconds since the Unix epoch. */
type Timestamp = number

/** Evidence: a verbatim quote of at most 30 words from one block, justifying one IR element (§5, §16). `start`/`end` are filled by the grounding matcher (offsets into the block's text); `fuzzy` is set when the match needed the Levenshtein fallback (§16 step 4). */
type Evidence = {
    blockId: string;
    quote: string;
    start?: number | undefined;
    end?: number | undefined;
    fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
}

/** Reasoning effort sent on every model call. v1 allows only these three (§9). */
type ReasoningEffort = "low" | "medium" | "high"

/** Model tier a role runs on (§9). */
type Tier = "fast" | "standard" | "deep"

/** Which model and prompt version produced a record (`indexedWith`, `createdWith`, §5). */
type ProducedWith = {
    model: string;
    promptVersion: string;
}
```

### `src/schemas/concepts.ts`

```ts
/** Content flags the indexer sets per section (§5). */
type SectionFlags = {
    algorithm: boolean;
    protocol: boolean;
    architecture: boolean;
    dataStructure: boolean;
    stateMachine: boolean;
    math: boolean;
    codeHeavy: boolean;
    narrative: boolean;
}

/** What the `indexer` model returns for one section; the client adds `indexedWith`. */
type SectionIndexOutput = {
    sectionId: string;
    summary: string;
    defines: {
        name: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
    uses: {
        name: string;
    }[];
    flags: {
        algorithm: boolean;
        protocol: boolean;
        architecture: boolean;
        dataStructure: boolean;
        stateMachine: boolean;
        math: boolean;
        codeHeavy: boolean;
        narrative: boolean;
    };
}

/** Per-section concept index (§5). */
type SectionIndex = {
    sectionId: string;
    summary: string;
    defines: {
        name: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
    uses: {
        name: string;
    }[];
    flags: {
        algorithm: boolean;
        protocol: boolean;
        architecture: boolean;
        dataStructure: boolean;
        stateMachine: boolean;
        math: boolean;
        codeHeavy: boolean;
        narrative: boolean;
    };
    indexedWith: {
        model: string;
        promptVersion: string;
    };
}

/** Record of the `sectionIndex` store: a `SectionIndex` plus its `bookId` (store index, §5). */
type SectionIndexRecord = {
    sectionId: string;
    summary: string;
    defines: {
        name: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
    uses: {
        name: string;
    }[];
    flags: {
        algorithm: boolean;
        protocol: boolean;
        architecture: boolean;
        dataStructure: boolean;
        stateMachine: boolean;
        math: boolean;
        codeHeavy: boolean;
        narrative: boolean;
    };
    indexedWith: {
        model: string;
        promptVersion: string;
    };
    bookId: string;
}

/** Output of the `indexer` role: one entry per section in the batch (≤ 8 sections, §6 phase B step 3). Wrapped in an object because structured outputs need an object at the root (ADR 0003). */
type IndexerOutput = {
    sections: {
        sectionId: string;
        summary: string;
        defines: {
            name: string;
            evidence: {
                blockId: string;
                quote: string;
                start?: number | undefined;
                end?: number | undefined;
                fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
            };
        }[];
        uses: {
            name: string;
        }[];
        flags: {
            algorithm: boolean;
            protocol: boolean;
            architecture: boolean;
            dataStructure: boolean;
            stateMachine: boolean;
            math: boolean;
            codeHeavy: boolean;
            narrative: boolean;
        };
    }[];
}

/** A concept in the book-wide registry (§5). */
type Concept = {
    conceptId: string;
    bookId: string;
    canonical: string;
    aliases: string[];
    firstOrdinal: number;
    usedInSectionIds: string[];
    spanning: boolean;
    definedInSectionId?: string | undefined;
    definition?: {
        blockId: string;
        quote: string;
        start?: number | undefined;
        end?: number | undefined;
        fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
    } | undefined;
}

/** One merged cluster from the `glossary` role: a canonical name and its aliases (§6 phase B step 5). */
type ConceptMerge = {
    canonical: string;
    aliases: string[];
}

/** Output of the `glossary` role (`ConceptMerge[]` wrapped in an object, ADR 0003). */
type GlossaryOutput = {
    merges: {
        canonical: string;
        aliases: string[];
    }[];
}
```

```ts
        label: string;
        attributes: {
            name: string;
            evidence: {
                blockId: string;
                quote: string;
                start?: number | undefined;
                end?: number | undefined;
                fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
            };
            type?: string | undefined;
            key?: "PK" | "FK" | "UK" | undefined;
        }[];
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
    relationships: {
        id: string;
        from: string;
        to: string;
        fromCardinality: "zero-or-one" | "exactly-one" | "zero-or-more" | "one-or-more";
        toCardinality: "zero-or-one" | "exactly-one" | "zero-or-more" | "one-or-more";
        label: string;
        identifying: boolean;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
}

/** Evidence for one element of a template's props, addressed by element path, e.g. 'nodes.3' (§5). */
type TemplateEvidence = {
    path: string;
    evidence: {
        blockId: string;
        quote: string;
        start?: number | undefined;
        end?: number | undefined;
        fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
    };
}

/** Data-structure template instance (§5, §10). `props` is validated by the template's own schema from the template registry. `evidence` is a list keyed by element path instead of the spec's `Record<string, Evidence>` so it fits strict structured outputs (ADR 0003). */
type TemplateIR = {
    type: "template";
    templateId: "array" | "linked-list" | "stack" | "queue" | "hash-table" | "tree" | "graph-ds" | "grid" | "memory" | "bits" | "call-tree" | "pipeline" | "layers" | "cache" | "comparison" | "set-ops";
    props: unknown;
    evidence: {
        path: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
}

type ChartFn = "constant" | "log" | "linear" | "nlogn" | "quadratic" | "cubic" | "exponential"

type ChartSeries = {
    name: string;
    evidence: {
        blockId: string;
        quote: string;
        start?: number | undefined;
        end?: number | undefined;
        fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
    };
    fn?: "constant" | "log" | "linear" | "nlogn" | "quadratic" | "cubic" | "exponential" | undefined;
    points?: [number, number][] | undefined;
}

/** Growth rates and numbers stated in the text; compiled to Vega-Lite (§8, §10). ≤ 4 series. */
type ChartIR = {
    type: "chart";
    title: string;
    xLabel: string;
    yLabel: string;
    domain: [number, number];
    series: {
        name: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
        fn?: "constant" | "log" | "linear" | "nlogn" | "quadratic" | "cubic" | "exponential" | undefined;
        points?: [number, number][] | undefined;
    }[];
    logY: boolean;
}

type MathTerm = {
    symbol: string;
    meaning: string;
    evidence: {
        blockId: string;
        quote: string;
        start?: number | undefined;
        end?: number | undefined;
        fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
    };
}

/** Term meanings for a formula; the formula itself always comes from the book block (§5, §10). */
type MathIR = {
    type: "math";
    sourceBlockId: string;
    terms: {
        symbol: string;
        meaning: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
}

type TimelineLane = {
    id: string;
    label: string;
}

type TimelineEvent = {
    id: string;
    lane: string;
    at: number;
    label: string;
    evidence: {
        blockId: string;
        quote: string;
        start?: number | undefined;
        end?: number | undefined;
        fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
    };
    to?: string | undefined;
}

/** Concurrency lanes and events; always a simulated timeline (§2 D9, §10). */
type TimelineIR = {
    type: "timeline";
    simulated: true;
    lanes: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
    }[];
    events: {
        id: string;
        lane: string;
        at: number;
        label: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
        to?: string | undefined;
    }[];
}

/** Last-resort self-contained HTML explainer, behind the `htmlFallback` flag only (§10, §17). */
type HtmlIR = {
    type: "html";
    html: string;
    reason: string;
}

/** Any extractor IR, discriminated by `type`. `trace` visuals use `Trace` instead of an IR. */
type IR = {
    type: "graph";
    title: string;
    direction: "LR" | "TB";
    nodes: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
        shape: "queue" | "box" | "store" | "actor" | "decision" | "note";
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
        sublabel?: string | undefined;
        group?: string | undefined;
    }[];
    edges: {
        id: string;
        from: string;
        to: string;
        style: "solid" | "dashed";
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
        label?: string | undefined;
    }[];
    groups: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
        parent?: string | undefined;
    }[];
} | {
    type: "sequence";
    title: string;
    participants: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
    messages: {
        id: string;
        from: string;
        to: string;
        label: string;
        kind: "sync" | "async" | "reply";
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
} | {
    type: "state";
    title: string;
    initial: string;
    final: string[];
    states: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
    transitions: {
        id: string;
        from: string;
        to: string;
        event: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
        guard?: string | undefined;
    }[];
} | {
    type: "class";
    title: string;
    classes: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
        fields: {
            name: string;
            evidence: {
                blockId: string;
                quote: string;
                start?: number | undefined;
                end?: number | undefined;
                fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
            };
            type?: string | undefined;
            visibility?: "+" | "-" | "#" | "~" | undefined;
            isStatic?: boolean | undefined;
        }[];
        methods: {
            name: string;
            evidence: {
                blockId: string;
                quote: string;
                start?: number | undefined;
                end?: number | undefined;
                fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
            };
            params?: string | undefined;
            returns?: string | undefined;
            visibility?: "+" | "-" | "#" | "~" | undefined;
            isStatic?: boolean | undefined;
            isAbstract?: boolean | undefined;
        }[];
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
        stereotype?: "interface" | "abstract" | "enumeration" | "service" | undefined;
    }[];
    relations: {
        id: string;
        from: string;
        to: string;
        kind: "inheritance" | "realization" | "composition" | "aggregation" | "association" | "dependency";
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
        label?: string | undefined;
        fromCardinality?: "1" | "0..1" | "0..*" | "1..*" | "*" | "n" | undefined;
        toCardinality?: "1" | "0..1" | "0..*" | "1..*" | "*" | "n" | undefined;
    }[];
} | {
    type: "er";
    title: string;
    entities: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
        attributes: {
            name: string;
            evidence: {
                blockId: string;
                quote: string;
                start?: number | undefined;
                end?: number | undefined;
                fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
            };
            type?: string | undefined;
            key?: "PK" | "FK" | "UK" | undefined;
        }[];
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
    relationships: {
        id: string;
        from: string;
        to: string;
        fromCardinality: "zero-or-one" | "exactly-one" | "zero-or-more" | "one-or-more";
        toCardinality: "zero-or-one" | "exactly-one" | "zero-or-more" | "one-or-more";
        label: string;
        identifying: boolean;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
} | {
    type: "template";
    templateId: "array" | "linked-list" | "stack" | "queue" | "hash-table" | "tree" | "graph-ds" | "grid" | "memory" | "bits" | "call-tree" | "pipeline" | "layers" | "cache" | "comparison" | "set-ops";
    props: unknown;
    evidence: {
        path: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
} | {
    type: "chart";
    title: string;
    xLabel: string;
    yLabel: string;
    domain: [number, number];
    series: {
        name: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
        fn?: "constant" | "log" | "linear" | "nlogn" | "quadratic" | "cubic" | "exponential" | undefined;
        points?: [number, number][] | undefined;
    }[];
    logY: boolean;
} | {
    type: "math";
    sourceBlockId: string;
    terms: {
        symbol: string;
        meaning: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
    }[];
} | {
    type: "timeline";
    simulated: true;
    lanes: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
    }[];
    events: {
        id: string;
        lane: string;
        at: number;
        label: string;
        evidence: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        };
        to?: string | undefined;
    }[];
} | {
    type: "html";
    html: string;
    reason: string;
}

/** IR kinds (every `VisualKind` except `trace`). */
type IrKind = "math" | "html" | "graph" | "sequence" | "state" | "class" | "er" | "template" | "chart" | "timeline"
```

### `src/schemas/llm.ts`

```ts
type SchemaName = "IndexerOutput" | "GlossaryOutput" | "BookSummary" | "VisualPlan" | "GraphIR" | "SequenceIR" | "StateIR" | "ClassIR" | "ErIR" | "TemplateIR" | "ChartIR" | "MathIR" | "TimelineIR" | "HtmlIR" | "AnimationIR" | "Translation" | "PatchOutput" | "CriticReport"

type RoleName = "critic" | "indexer" | "glossary" | "book-summary" | "planner" | "extractor.graph" | "extractor.mermaid" | "extractor.template" | "extractor.chart" | "extractor.math" | "extractor.timeline" | "animator" | "translator" | "trace-fixer" | "patcher" | "repair" | "html-fallback"

type LlmImage = {
    mediaType: "image/png";
    base64: string;
}

/** Request body of `POST /api/llm` (§9). */
type LlmRequest = {
    role: "critic" | "indexer" | "glossary" | "book-summary" | "planner" | "extractor.graph" | "extractor.mermaid" | "extractor.template" | "extractor.chart" | "extractor.math" | "extractor.timeline" | "animator" | "translator" | "trace-fixer" | "patcher" | "repair" | "html-fallback";
    promptVersion: string;
    prefix: string;
    body: string;
    schemaName: "IndexerOutput" | "GlossaryOutput" | "BookSummary" | "VisualPlan" | "GraphIR" | "SequenceIR" | "StateIR" | "ClassIR" | "ErIR" | "TemplateIR" | "ChartIR" | "MathIR" | "TimelineIR" | "HtmlIR" | "AnimationIR" | "Translation" | "PatchOutput" | "CriticReport";
    requestId: string;
    templateId?: "array" | "linked-list" | "stack" | "queue" | "hash-table" | "tree" | "graph-ds" | "grid" | "memory" | "bits" | "call-tree" | "pipeline" | "layers" | "cache" | "comparison" | "set-ops" | undefined;
    image?: {
        mediaType: "image/png";
        base64: string;
    } | undefined;
    escalate?: boolean | undefined;
}

type LlmUsage = {
    input: number;
    output: number;
    cachedInput: number;
}

/** Error codes of the gateway (§9). */
type LlmErrorCode = "schema" | "auth" | "signin-required" | "plan-limit" | "upstream" | "timeout"

/** Response body of `POST /api/llm` (§9). `effort` is the effort actually sent (ADR 0003). */
type LlmResponse = {
    ok: true;
    data: unknown;
    usage: {
        input: number;
        output: number;
        cachedInput: number;
    };
    model: string;
    effort: "low" | "medium" | "high";
    latencyMs: number;
} | {
    ok: false;
    code: "schema" | "auth" | "signin-required" | "plan-limit" | "upstream" | "timeout";
    message: string;
    retryAfterMs?: number | undefined;
    issues?: unknown[] | undefined;
}
```

### `src/schemas/plan.ts`

```ts
/** The visual families a plan item can request (§5, kind guide in §8). */
type VisualKind = "math" | "html" | "graph" | "sequence" | "state" | "class" | "er" | "template" | "chart" | "trace" | "timeline"

/** The 16 templates of the template library (§10). */
type TemplateId = "array" | "linked-list" | "stack" | "queue" | "hash-table" | "tree" | "graph-ds" | "grid" | "memory" | "bits" | "call-tree" | "pipeline" | "layers" | "cache" | "comparison" | "set-ops"

/** Kinds that may extend or start a concept model in v1 (§13). */
type ConceptModelKind = "graph" | "sequence" | "state"

/** One visual the planner wants for a section (§5). */
type PlanItem = {
    planItemId: string;
    kind: "math" | "html" | "graph" | "sequence" | "state" | "class" | "er" | "template" | "chart" | "trace" | "timeline";
    intent: string;
    conceptIds: string[];
    priority: 1 | 3 | 2;
    animate: boolean;
    templateId?: "array" | "linked-list" | "stack" | "queue" | "hash-table" | "tree" | "graph-ds" | "grid" | "memory" | "bits" | "call-tree" | "pipeline" | "layers" | "cache" | "comparison" | "set-ops" | undefined;
    codeBlockId?: string | undefined;
    conceptModelId?: string | undefined;
    newConceptModel?: string | undefined;
}

/** Output of the `planner` role (§5, §8 stage 1): at most 3 items, none when no visual is needed. */
type VisualPlan = {
    sectionId: string;
    needsVisual: boolean;
    reason: string;
    items: {
        planItemId: string;
        kind: "math" | "html" | "graph" | "sequence" | "state" | "class" | "er" | "template" | "chart" | "trace" | "timeline";
        intent: string;
        conceptIds: string[];
        priority: 1 | 3 | 2;
        animate: boolean;
        templateId?: "array" | "linked-list" | "stack" | "queue" | "hash-table" | "tree" | "graph-ds" | "grid" | "memory" | "bits" | "call-tree" | "pipeline" | "layers" | "cache" | "comparison" | "set-ops" | undefined;
        codeBlockId?: string | undefined;
        conceptModelId?: string | undefined;
        newConceptModel?: string | undefined;
    }[];
}

/** Record of the `plans` store (§5), keyed by the plan cache key. */
type PlanRecord = {
    cacheKey: string;
    bookId: string;
    sectionId: string;
    plan: {
        sectionId: string;
        needsVisual: boolean;
        reason: string;
        items: {
            planItemId: string;
            kind: "math" | "html" | "graph" | "sequence" | "state" | "class" | "er" | "template" | "chart" | "trace" | "timeline";
            intent: string;
            conceptIds: string[];
            priority: 1 | 3 | 2;
            animate: boolean;
            templateId?: "array" | "linked-list" | "stack" | "queue" | "hash-table" | "tree" | "graph-ds" | "grid" | "memory" | "bits" | "call-tree" | "pipeline" | "layers" | "cache" | "comparison" | "set-ops" | undefined;
            codeBlockId?: string | undefined;
            conceptModelId?: string | undefined;
            newConceptModel?: string | undefined;
        }[];
    };
    partialContext: boolean;
    model: string;
    effort: "low" | "medium" | "high";
    promptVersion: string;
    usageTokens: number;
    createdAt: number;
    stale: boolean;
}
```

### `src/schemas/ports.ts`

```ts
/** Theme tokens derived from Flow's active theme, exposed as `--illus-*` CSS variables (§10). */
type ThemeTokens = {
    bg: string;
    fg: string;
    muted: string;
    line: string;
    accent: string;
    new: string;
    earlier: string;
    highlight: string;
    font: string;
    mono: string;
    dark: boolean;
}

type GroundingStatus = "grounded" | "half-grounded" | "failed"

/** The grounding matcher (§16). Pure and synchronous. Implemented by `ground()` in `@illus/core/grounding`. */
type GroundingFn = (evidence: Evidence, ctx: GroundingContext) => GroundingResult

/** Schema + grounding + structural checks for one IR (§16 L1–L3). Implemented by `validate()` (W10). */
type Validator = (kind: VisualKind, ir: unknown, ctx: ValidationContext) => ValidationResult

/** ELK layout in a Web Worker (§10). Implemented by `layoutGraph()` in `@illus/render`. */
type LayoutGraphFn = (input: LayoutGraphInput, opts?: {
    signal?: AbortSignal;
}) => Promise<LayoutGraphResult>
```

### `src/schemas/quality.ts`

```ts
/** Validation layer that raised an issue (§16 table: L1 schema … L4 render; animation is §11). */
type ValidationLayer = "schema" | "grounding" | "structure" | "render" | "animation"

type ValidationIssue = {
    layer: "schema" | "grounding" | "structure" | "render" | "animation";
    severity: "error" | "warning";
    code: string;
    message: string;
    elementId?: string | undefined;
    autoFixed?: boolean | undefined;
}

/** Outcome of the deterministic checks on one IR (§16 L1–L4). Not defined in the spec; W0 draft. `groundingScore` = (grounded + 0.5 × halfGrounded) / total (§16). */
type ValidationReport = {
    ok: boolean;
    groundingScore: number;
    counts: {
        total: number;
        grounded: number;
        halfGrounded: number;
        fuzzy: number;
        dropped: number;
    };
    dropped: string[];
    issues: {
        layer: "schema" | "grounding" | "structure" | "render" | "animation";
        severity: "error" | "warning";
        code: string;
        message: string;
        elementId?: string | undefined;
        autoFixed?: boolean | undefined;
    }[];
    retried: boolean;
    escalated: boolean;
}

/** Output of the `critic` role (§16). */
type CriticReport = {
    pass: boolean;
    scores: {
        legibility: 1 | 3 | 4 | 2 | 5;
        intentMatch: 1 | 3 | 4 | 2 | 5;
        clutter: 1 | 3 | 4 | 2 | 5;
    };
    issues: {
        kind: "clutter" | "overlap" | "unreadable" | "missing-step" | "wrong-direction" | "mislabel" | "other";
        note: string;
        elementId?: string | undefined;
    }[];
}
```

### `src/schemas/records.ts`

```ts
/** Element-level "This is wrong" report from the pane (§16 human feedback). */
type ElementReport = {
    elementId: string;
    note: string;
    createdAt: number;
    evidence?: {
        blockId: string;
        quote: string;
        start?: number | undefined;
        end?: number | undefined;
        fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
    } | undefined;
}

/** Record of the `visuals` store (§5), keyed by the visual cache key. */
type VisualRecord = {
    cacheKey: string;
    bookId: string;
    sectionId: string;
    planItemId: string;
    kind: "math" | "html" | "graph" | "sequence" | "state" | "class" | "er" | "template" | "chart" | "trace" | "timeline";
    ir: unknown;
    spec: unknown;
    validation: {
        ok: boolean;
        groundingScore: number;
        counts: {
            total: number;
            grounded: number;
            halfGrounded: number;
            fuzzy: number;
            dropped: number;
        };
        dropped: string[];
        issues: {
            layer: "schema" | "grounding" | "structure" | "render" | "animation";
            severity: "error" | "warning";
            code: string;
            message: string;
            elementId?: string | undefined;
            autoFixed?: boolean | undefined;
        }[];
        retried: boolean;
        escalated: boolean;
    };
    promptVersions: Record<string, string>;
    models: Record<string, string>;
    usageTokens: number;
    createdAt: number;
    stale: boolean;
    animation?: {
        planItemId: string;
        initialHidden: (`node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`)[];
        steps: {
            id: string;
            narration: string;
            evidence: {
                blockId: string;
                quote: string;
                start?: number | undefined;
                end?: number | undefined;
                fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
            };
            actions: ({
                op: "highlight";
                target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
                tone: "success" | "focus" | "warning" | "muted";
            } | {
                op: "unhighlight";
                target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | "all";
            } | {
                op: "reveal";
                target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
            } | {
                op: "hide";
                target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
            } | {
                op: "set";
                target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
                value: string | number;
            } | {
                op: "swap";
                a: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
                b: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
            } | {
                op: "pointer";
                name: string;
                target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | null;
            } | {
                op: "push" | "enqueue";
                container: string;
                id: string;
                value: string | number;
            } | {
                op: "pop" | "dequeue";
                container: string;
            } | {
                op: "add";
                element: {
                    id: string;
                    label: string;
                };
                parent?: string | undefined;
            } | {
                op: "remove";
                target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
            } | {
                op: "token";
                id: string;
                path: string[];
                label?: string | undefined;
            } | {
                op: "annotate";
                target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}`;
                text: string;
            } | {
                op: "focus";
                target: `node:${string}` | `edge:${string}` | `group:${string}` | `msg:${string}` | `cell:${string}[${number}]` | `item:${string}` | "all";
            })[];
        }[];
    } | undefined;
    traceKey?: string | undefined;
    layout?: unknown;
    critic?: {
        pass: boolean;
        scores: {
            legibility: 1 | 3 | 4 | 2 | 5;
            intentMatch: 1 | 3 | 4 | 2 | 5;
            clutter: 1 | 3 | 4 | 2 | 5;
        };
        issues: {
            kind: "clutter" | "overlap" | "unreadable" | "missing-step" | "wrong-direction" | "mislabel" | "other";
            note: string;
            elementId?: string | undefined;
        }[];
    } | undefined;
    pinned?: boolean | undefined;
    instruction?: string | undefined;
    feedback?: {
        vote: "up" | "down";
        note?: string | undefined;
    } | undefined;
    elementReports?: {
        elementId: string;
        note: string;
        createdAt: number;
        evidence?: {
            blockId: string;
            quote: string;
            start?: number | undefined;
            end?: number | undefined;
            fuzzy?: boolean | undefined;
        } | undefined;
    }[] | undefined;
}

/** Outcome of one model call: `ok` or the gateway error code (§9). */
type LlmOutcome = "schema" | "ok" | "auth" | "signin-required" | "plan-limit" | "upstream" | "timeout" | "aborted"

/** Row of the `llmLog` store (§9 client): one per call. Prompts only when `debugPrompts` is on. */
type LlmLogEntry = {
    requestId: string;
    role: "critic" | "indexer" | "glossary" | "book-summary" | "planner" | "extractor.graph" | "extractor.mermaid" | "extractor.template" | "extractor.chart" | "extractor.math" | "extractor.timeline" | "animator" | "translator" | "trace-fixer" | "patcher" | "repair" | "html-fallback";
    promptVersion: string;
    model: string;
    effort: "low" | "medium" | "high";
    inputTokens: number;
    outputTokens: number;
    cachedInputTokens: number;
    latencyMs: number;
    outcome: "schema" | "ok" | "auth" | "signin-required" | "plan-limit" | "upstream" | "timeout" | "aborted";
    createdAt: number;
    id?: number | undefined;
    bookId?: string | undefined;
    sectionId?: string | undefined;
    prompt?: {
        prefix: string;
        body: string;
    } | undefined;
}

/** Row of the `settings` store (§5): a key and a JSON value. */
type SettingRecord = {
    key: string;
    value: unknown;
}
```

### `src/schemas/trace.ts`

```ts
/** Output of the `translator` and `trace-fixer` roles (§12). */
type Translation = {
    traceable: boolean;
    python: string;
    scenarios: {
        label: string;
        entry: string;
    }[];
    inputRationale: string;
    lineMap: {
        py: number;
        orig: number[];
    }[];
    focusFunctions: string[];
    vizHints: {
        variable: string;
        template: "array" | "linked-list" | "stack" | "queue" | "hash-table" | "tree" | "graph-ds" | "grid" | "memory" | "bits" | "call-tree" | "pipeline" | "layers" | "cache" | "comparison" | "set-ops";
        fn?: string | undefined;
        pointerOf?: string | undefined;
    }[];
    assumptions: string[];
    reason?: string | undefined;
}

/** A primitive value or a reference into the step's heap (§12). */
type ValueRef = {
    prim: string | number | boolean | null;
} | {
    ref: string;
}

/** A heap object reachable from locals, identified by Python `id()` (§12). */
type HeapObj = {
    kind: "object" | "list" | "tuple" | "set" | "dict";
    cls?: string | undefined;
    items?: ({
        prim: string | number | boolean | null;
    } | {
        ref: string;
    })[] | undefined;
    entries?: [{
        prim: string | number | boolean | null;
    } | {
        ref: string;
    }, {
        prim: string | number | boolean | null;
    } | {
        ref: string;
    }][] | undefined;
    fields?: Record<string, {
        prim: string | number | boolean | null;
    } | {
        ref: string;
    }> | undefined;
    truncated?: boolean | undefined;
}

/** One recorded execution event (§12). Frames are innermost last. */
type TraceStep = {
    i: number;
    event: "line" | "call" | "return" | "exception";
    pyLine: number;
    origLines: number[];
    frames: {
        fn: string;
        locals: Record<string, {
            prim: string | number | boolean | null;
        } | {
            ref: string;
        }>;
    }[];
    heap: Record<string, {
        kind: "object" | "list" | "tuple" | "set" | "dict";
        cls?: string | undefined;
        items?: ({
            prim: string | number | boolean | null;
        } | {
            ref: string;
        })[] | undefined;
        entries?: [{
            prim: string | number | boolean | null;
        } | {
            ref: string;
        }, {
            prim: string | number | boolean | null;
        } | {
            ref: string;
        }][] | undefined;
        fields?: Record<string, {
            prim: string | number | boolean | null;
        } | {
            ref: string;
        }> | undefined;
        truncated?: boolean | undefined;
    }>;
    stdout: string;
    returnValue?: {
        prim: string | number | boolean | null;
    } | {
        ref: string;
    } | undefined;
    exception?: string | undefined;
    skipped?: {
        iterations: number;
    } | undefined;
}

/** What the trace worker returns for one run, before compression (§12 trace worker API). */
type RawTrace = {
    steps: {
        i: number;
        event: "line" | "call" | "return" | "exception";
        pyLine: number;
        origLines: number[];
        frames: {
            fn: string;
            locals: Record<string, {
                prim: string | number | boolean | null;
            } | {
                ref: string;
            }>;
        }[];
        heap: Record<string, {
            kind: "object" | "list" | "tuple" | "set" | "dict";
            cls?: string | undefined;
            items?: ({
                prim: string | number | boolean | null;
            } | {
                ref: string;
            })[] | undefined;
            entries?: [{
                prim: string | number | boolean | null;
            } | {
                ref: string;
            }, {
                prim: string | number | boolean | null;
            } | {
                ref: string;
            }][] | undefined;
            fields?: Record<string, {
                prim: string | number | boolean | null;
            } | {
                ref: string;
            }> | undefined;
            truncated?: boolean | undefined;
        }>;
        stdout: string;
        returnValue?: {
            prim: string | number | boolean | null;
        } | {
            ref: string;
        } | undefined;
        exception?: string | undefined;
        skipped?: {
            iterations: number;
        } | undefined;
    }[];
    truncated: boolean;
    error?: string | undefined;
}

/** A compressed, stored trace of one scenario (§5, §12). `input` is the scenario's entry call. */
type Trace = {
    codeBlockId: string;
    python: string;
    lineMap: {
        py: number;
        orig: number[];
    }[];
    input: string;
    assumptions: string[];
    steps: {
        i: number;
        event: "line" | "call" | "return" | "exception";
        pyLine: number;
        origLines: number[];
        frames: {
            fn: string;
            locals: Record<string, {
                prim: string | number | boolean | null;
            } | {
                ref: string;
            }>;
        }[];
        heap: Record<string, {
            kind: "object" | "list" | "tuple" | "set" | "dict";
            cls?: string | undefined;
            items?: ({
                prim: string | number | boolean | null;
            } | {
                ref: string;
            })[] | undefined;
            entries?: [{
                prim: string | number | boolean | null;
            } | {
                ref: string;
            }, {
                prim: string | number | boolean | null;
            } | {
                ref: string;
            }][] | undefined;
            fields?: Record<string, {
                prim: string | number | boolean | null;
            } | {
                ref: string;
            }> | undefined;
            truncated?: boolean | undefined;
        }>;
        stdout: string;
        returnValue?: {
            prim: string | number | boolean | null;
        } | {
            ref: string;
        } | undefined;
        exception?: string | undefined;
        skipped?: {
            iterations: number;
        } | undefined;
    }[];
    truncated: boolean;
    error?: string | undefined;
}

/** A trace with the label of the scenario it ran (scenario tabs in the trace view). */
type ScenarioTrace = {
    label: string;
    trace: {
        codeBlockId: string;
        python: string;
        lineMap: {
            py: number;
            orig: number[];
        }[];
        input: string;
        assumptions: string[];
        steps: {
            i: number;
            event: "line" | "call" | "return" | "exception";
            pyLine: number;
            origLines: number[];
            frames: {
                fn: string;
                locals: Record<string, {
                    prim: string | number | boolean | null;
                } | {
                    ref: string;
                }>;
            }[];
            heap: Record<string, {
                kind: "object" | "list" | "tuple" | "set" | "dict";
                cls?: string | undefined;
                items?: ({
                    prim: string | number | boolean | null;
                } | {
                    ref: string;
                })[] | undefined;
                entries?: [{
                    prim: string | number | boolean | null;
                } | {
                    ref: string;
                }, {
                    prim: string | number | boolean | null;
                } | {
                    ref: string;
                }][] | undefined;
                fields?: Record<string, {
                    prim: string | number | boolean | null;
                } | {
                    ref: string;
                }> | undefined;
                truncated?: boolean | undefined;
            }>;
            stdout: string;
            returnValue?: {
                prim: string | number | boolean | null;
            } | {
                ref: string;
            } | undefined;
            exception?: string | undefined;
            skipped?: {
                iterations: number;
            } | undefined;
        }[];
        truncated: boolean;
        error?: string | undefined;
    };
}

/** Record of the `traces` store (§5), keyed by the trace cache key. */
type TraceRecord = {
    cacheKey: string;
    bookId: string;
    codeBlockId: string;
    translation: {
        traceable: boolean;
        python: string;
        scenarios: {
            label: string;
            entry: string;
        }[];
        inputRationale: string;
        lineMap: {
            py: number;
            orig: number[];
        }[];
        focusFunctions: string[];
        vizHints: {
            variable: string;
            template: "array" | "linked-list" | "stack" | "queue" | "hash-table" | "tree" | "graph-ds" | "grid" | "memory" | "bits" | "call-tree" | "pipeline" | "layers" | "cache" | "comparison" | "set-ops";
            fn?: string | undefined;
            pointerOf?: string | undefined;
        }[];
        assumptions: string[];
        reason?: string | undefined;
    };
    scenarios: {
        label: string;
        trace: {
            codeBlockId: string;
            python: string;
            lineMap: {
                py: number;
                orig: number[];
            }[];
            input: string;
            assumptions: string[];
            steps: {
                i: number;
                event: "line" | "call" | "return" | "exception";
                pyLine: number;
                origLines: number[];
                frames: {
                    fn: string;
                    locals: Record<string, {
                        prim: string | number | boolean | null;
                    } | {
                        ref: string;
                    }>;
                }[];
                heap: Record<string, {
                    kind: "object" | "list" | "tuple" | "set" | "dict";
                    cls?: string | undefined;
                    items?: ({
                        prim: string | number | boolean | null;
                    } | {
                        ref: string;
                    })[] | undefined;
                    entries?: [{
                        prim: string | number | boolean | null;
                    } | {
                        ref: string;
                    }, {
                        prim: string | number | boolean | null;
                    } | {
                        ref: string;
                    }][] | undefined;
                    fields?: Record<string, {
                        prim: string | number | boolean | null;
                    } | {

                    }> | undefined;
                    truncated?: boolean | undefined;
                }>;
                stdout: string;
                returnValue?: {

                } | {

                } | undefined;
                exception?: string | undefined;
                skipped?: {

                } | undefined;
            }[];

    }[];
    mode: "simulated" | "executed";
    promptVersions: Record<string, string>;
    models: Record<string, string>;

}
```

## 2. Model output schemas

Every `SchemaName` resolved by `/api/llm`. Properties missing from `required` are optional.

### IndexerOutput

<details><summary>JSON Schema</summary>

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "sections": {
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 8,
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "sectionId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1
          },
          "summary": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1
          },
          "defines": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "type": "object",
              "properties": {
                "name": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "minLength": 1
                },
                "evidence": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "properties": {
                    "blockId": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1
                    },
                    "quote": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1
                    },
                    "start": {
                      "type": "integer",
                      "minimum": 0,
                      "maximum": 9007199254740991
                    },
                    "end": {
                      "type": "integer",
                      "minimum": 0,
                      "maximum": 9007199254740991
                    },
                    "fuzzy": {
                      "type": "boolean"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "blockId",
                    "quote"
                  ],
                  "additionalProperties": false
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "name",
                "evidence"
              ],
              "additionalProperties": false
            }
          },
          "uses": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "type": "object",
              "properties": {
                "name": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "minLength": 1
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "name"
              ],
              "additionalProperties": false
            }
          },
          "flags": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
              "algorithm": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "protocol": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "architecture": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "dataStructure": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "stateMachine": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "math": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "codeHeavy": {
                "type": "boolean"
              },
              "narrative": {
                "type": "boolean"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "algorithm",
              "protocol",
              "architecture",
              "dataStructure",
              "stateMachine",
              "math",
              "codeHeavy",
              "narrative"
            ],
            "additionalProperties": false
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "sectionId",
          "summary",
          "defines",
          "uses",
          "flags"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": false
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "sections"
  ],
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## 3. API reference

## @illus/core

### Classes

#### LlmError

Error thrown by `LlmCall.call`. `aborted` is client-side only (the caller's AbortSignal fired).

##### Extends

- `Error`

##### Constructors

###### Constructor

```ts
new LlmError(
   code,
   message,
   extra?
): LlmError;
```

####### Parameters

| Parameter | Type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `code` | \| `"schema"` \| `"auth"` \| `"signin-required"` \| `"plan-limit"` \| `"upstream"` \| `"timeout"` \| `"aborted"` |
| `message` | `string` |
| `extra` | \{ `issues?`: `unknown`[]; `retryAfterMs?`: `number`; \} |
| `extra.issues?` | `unknown`[] |
| `extra.retryAfterMs?` | `number` |

####### Returns

[`LlmError`](#llmerror)

####### Overrides

```ts
Error.constructor
```

##### Properties

###### cause?

```ts
optional cause?: unknown;
```

####### Inherited from

```ts
Error.cause
```

###### code

```ts
readonly code:
  | "schema"
  | "auth"
  | "signin-required"
  | "plan-limit"
  | "upstream"
  | "timeout"
  | "aborted";
```

###### issues

```ts
readonly issues: unknown[] | undefined;
```

###### message

```ts
message: string;
```

####### Inherited from

```ts
Error.message
```

###### name

```ts
name: string;
```

####### Inherited from

```ts
Error.name
```

###### retryAfterMs

```ts
readonly retryAfterMs: number | undefined;
```

###### stack?

```ts
optional stack?: string;
```

####### Inherited from

```ts
Error.stack
```

#### AnimationOptions

##### Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| <a id="property-grounding"></a> `grounding?` | [`GroundingContext`](#groundingcontext) | Mandatory for validateAnimation; only permitted section blocks may be supplied. |
| <a id="property-templates"></a> `templates?` | [`TemplateRegistry`](#templateregistry)\<[`TemplateSpec`](#templatespec)\<`unknown`\>\> | - |

***

##### Properties

| Property | Type | Description |
| ------ | ------ | ------ |
| <a id="property-animation"></a> `animation?` | `object` | - |
| `animation.initialHidden` | ( \| `` `node:${string}` `` \| `` `edge:${string}` `` \| `` `group:${string}` `` \| `` `msg:${string}` `` \| `` `cell:${string}[${number}]` `` \| `` `item:${string}` ``)[] | Elements revealed by later steps (progressive build-up). |
| `animation.planItemId` | `string` | - |
| `animation.steps` | `object`[] | - |

| <a id="property-issues"></a> `issues` | `object`[] | - |
| <a id="property-ok"></a> `ok` | `boolean` | - |

***

#### BlocksRepo

Basic keyed access shared by every store. `put` upserts.

##### Extends

- [`KeyedRepo`](#keyedrepo)\<[`Block`](#block)\>.[`PerBook`](#perbook)

##### Methods

###### bulkGet()

```ts
bulkGet(keys): Promise<(
  | {
  blockId: string;
  cfiRange: string;
  code?: {
     langHint?: string;
     source: string;
  };
  figure?: {
     alt?: string;
     caption?: string;
     imgHref?: string;
  };
  kind:   | "quote"
     | "paragraph"
     | "code"
     | "figure"
     | "table"
     | "math"
     | "list"
     | "aside";
  label?: string;
  math?: {
     mathml?: string;
     tex?: string;
  };
  sectionId: string;
  table?: {
     html: string;
     rows: string[][];
  };
  text: string;
}
| undefined)[]>;
```

####### Parameters

| Parameter | Type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `keys` | readonly `string`[] |

####### Returns

`Promise`\<(
  \| \{
  `blockId`: `string`;
  `cfiRange`: `string`;
  `code?`: \{
     `langHint?`: `string`;
     `source`: `string`;
  \};
  `figure?`: \{
     `alt?`: `string`;
     `caption?`: `string`;
     `imgHref?`: `string`;
  \};
  `kind`:   \| `"quote"`
     \| `"paragraph"`

#### LlmCall

Typed model call (§9 client). Rejects with `LlmError` (`plan-limit`, `signin-required`, `schema`, …).
Implemented by `@illus/llm`; replay mode serves fixtures only.

##### Methods

###### call()

```ts
call<R, S>(
   role,
   ctx,
   opts?
): Promise<LlmResult<SchemaOutput<S>>>;
```

####### Type Parameters

| Type Parameter | Default type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `R` *extends* \| `"indexer"` \| `"glossary"` \| `"book-summary"` \| `"planner"` \| `"extractor.graph"` \| `"extractor.mermaid"` \| `"extractor.template"` \| `"extractor.chart"` \| `"extractor.math"` \| `"extractor.timeline"` \| `"animator"` \| `"translator"` \| `"trace-fixer"` \| `"patcher"` \| `"critic"` \| `"repair"` \| `"html-fallback"` | - |
| `S` *extends* \| `"IndexerOutput"` \| `"GlossaryOutput"` \| `"BookSummary"` \| `"VisualPlan"` \| `"GraphIR"` \| `"SequenceIR"` \| `"StateIR"` \| `"ClassIR"` \| `"ErIR"` \| `"TemplateIR"` \| `"ChartIR"` \| `"MathIR"` \| `"TimelineIR"` \| `"HtmlIR"` \| `"AnimationIR"` \| `"Translation"` \| `"PatchOutput"` \| `"CriticReport"` | [`RoleSchemaName`](#roleschemaname)\<`R`\> |

####### Parameters

| Parameter | Type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `role` | `R` |
| `ctx` | [`LlmContext`](#llmcontext) |
| `opts?` | [`LlmCallOptions`](#llmcalloptions)\<`S`\> |

####### Returns

`Promise`\<[`LlmResult`](#llmresult)\<[`SchemaOutput`](#schemaoutput)\<`S`\>\>\>

#### TemplateSpec

The DOM-free part of a template (§10). `@illus/render` extends it with `layout(props)` and the React
`Component`.

##### Type Parameters

| Type Parameter | Default type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `P` | `unknown` |

##### Properties

| Property | Type |
| ------ | ------ |
| <a id="property-description"></a> `description` | `string` |
| <a id="property-example"></a> `example` | `P` |
| <a id="property-id"></a> `id` | \| `"array"` \| `"linked-list"` \| `"stack"` \| `"queue"` \| `"hash-table"` \| `"tree"` \| `"graph-ds"` \| `"grid"` \| `"memory"` \| `"bits"` \| `"call-tree"` \| `"pipeline"` \| `"layers"` \| `"cache"` \| `"comparison"` \| `"set-ops"` |
| <a id="property-propsschema"></a> `propsSchema` | `ZodType`\<`P`\> |
| <a id="property-supportedactions"></a> `supportedActions` | readonly ( \| `"set"` \| `"focus"` \| `"highlight"` \| `"unhighlight"` \| `"reveal"` \| `"hide"` \| `"swap"` \| `"pointer"` \| `"push"` \| `"enqueue"` \| `"pop"` \| `"dequeue"` \| `"add"` \| `"remove"` \| `"token"` \| `"annotate"`)[] |
| <a id="property-whentouse"></a> `whenToUse` | `string` |

##### Methods

###### checkInvariants()?

```ts
optional checkInvariants(props): string[];
```

Semantic invariants (BST order, heap order, pointers in range…); returns violation messages.

####### Parameters

| Parameter | Type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `props` | `P` |

####### Returns

`string`[]

###### dropElement()?

```ts
optional dropElement(props, path): P;
```

Removes the element at `path` (a `TemplateEvidence.path`, e.g. 'nodes.3') and returns new props, so
`validate()` can drop an ungrounded template element the way it does for other IR kinds (v1.1).
Must not mutate `props`. Without it, an ungrounded element fails the report instead.

####### Parameters

| Parameter | Type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `props` | `P` |
| `path` | `string` |

####### Returns

`P`

###### initialFrame()?

```ts
optional initialFrame(props): Partial<Pick<FrameState, "pointers" | "containers">>;
```

Seeds frame 0 with named pointers and full container contents (§11, v1.3). Container names used
by W5 are `stack`, `queue`, `frames` and `order`. Must not mutate or retain mutable aliases of
`props`; the reducer must also clone the seed before updating frames. Pure and DOM-free.

####### Parameters

| Parameter | Type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `props` | `P` |

####### Returns

`Partial`\<`Pick`\<[`FrameState`](#framestate), `"pointers"` \| `"containers"`\>\>

###### targets()?

```ts
optional targets(props): string[];
```

Every valid animation target for these props (§11, v1.3). Each string must parse as `Target`.
Positions stay addressable while `swap` exchanges their contents. Pure and DOM-free.

####### Parameters

| Parameter | Type |
| ------ | ------ |
| `props` | `P` |

####### Returns

`string`[]
